Abstract: 摘要

"Generating novel views from limited observations requires preserving both the appearance and spatial structure of a scene."
从有限观测中生成新视角需要同时保持场景的外观和空间结构。

"Although recent approaches exploit fine-grained geometric cues, they do not explicitly model high-level semantic and spatial properties such as object identity, attributes, and inter-object relationships."
尽管近期的方法利用了细粒度的几何线索，但它们并未显式地建模高层语义和空间属性，例如物体身份、属性以及物体间关系。

"In this work, we investigate whether 3D language understanding can provide complementary supervision for novel-view generation."
在本工作中，我们研究了3D语言理解能否为新视角生成提供互补的监督。

"We introduce Ask4NV, built on a pretrained unified multimodal model with a Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture."
我们提出了Ask4NV，它基于一个采用混合Transformer（Mixture-of-Transformers, MoT）架构的预训练统一多模态模型构建。

"Our model incorporates camera geometry into both VAE and ViT representations and leverages understanding features during generation through joint self-attention with a modality-aware attention masking strategy."
我们的模型将相机几何融入VAE和ViT表示中，并通过带有模态感知注意力掩码策略的联合自注意力机制，在生成过程中利用理解特征。

"Experiments demonstrate strong image quality and geometric consistency in novel-view generation, alongside competitive 3D language understanding."
实验表明，该模型在新视角生成中展现出强大的图像质量和几何一致性，同时具备具有竞争力的3D语言理解能力。

"Further analysis shows that joint training improves cross-view correspondence in VAE representations and similarity in a 3D-aware feature space."
进一步的分析表明，联合训练提升了VAE表示中的跨视角对应性以及3D感知特征空间中的相似性。

"Figure 1: We present Ask4NV, which enhances novel-view generation through 3D language understanding. Within a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture, 3D language understanding supervision trains the"
图1：我们提出了Ask4NV，它通过3D语言理解来增强新视角生成。在混合Transformer（Mixture-of-Transformers）架构中，3D语言理解监督训练...

"ow objects and their spatial arrangements should appear from a different viewpoint.
For example, recognizing a red chair beside a wooden table can guide the model to preserve the chair's identity, attributes, and relationship to the table, even as its visible shape and image position change with the viewpoint.
Learning these semantic and spatial properties could therefore complement fine-grained geometric information, helping the model preserve scene content and represent its spatial organization consistently across views.
However, the potential of explicitly learning such high-level properties for novel-view generation remains largely unexplored.
We then ask:
How can we provide such high-level semantic and spatial supervision, and how can it be incorporated into joint learning with novel-view generation?
We investigate this direction through 3D language understanding tasks
(
Hong et al., 2023
;
Huang et al., 2023
;
Zheng et al., 2025b
)
, which involve identifying and localizing objects and reasoning about their attributes and spatial relationships.
Fig.
2
highlights the shared semantic and spatial properties underlying 3D language understanding and novel-view generation, suggesting the potential of language-based supervision to support generation.
To jointly learn novel-view generation and 3D language understanding, we adopt pretrained unified multimodal models
(
Xie et al., 2025
;
Team, 2024
)
, which support both visual generation and multimodal understanding within a sin"

"Questions about object categories, attributes, and spatial relationships address the same scene properties that should be represented in novel views. "Concretely, we introduce Ask4NV, which builds on a pretrained Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture (Liang et al., 2024; Deng et al., 2025) and introduces a small set of architectural modifications to jointly model 3D language understanding and novel-view generation." - 具体而言，我们提出了 Ask4NV，它基于预训练的 Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) 架构（Liang et al., 2024; Deng et al., 2025），并引入少量架构修改，以联合建模 3D 语言理解与新视角生成。

"The MoT architecture provides task-specific parameters together with joint self-attention between the two transformers, allowing the two tasks to be learned within a shared framework without introducing a separate cross-task fusion module." - MoT 架构提供任务特定的参数，并在两个 Transformer 之间进行联合自注意力，使两个任务能够在共享框架内学习，而无需引入单独的跨任务融合模块。

"For novel-view generation, we use VAE latents alongside complementary ViT features from the understanding transformer and incorporate camera geometry into both representations through Plücker ray embeddings." - 在新视角生成中，我们使用 VAE 潜变量以及来自理解 Transformer 的互补 ViT 特征，并通过 Plücker 射线嵌入将相机几何信息融入这两种表示。

"We further design an attention masking strategy to facilitate information exchange across different token modalities during novel-view generation." - 我们还设计了一种注意力掩码策略，以促进新视角生成过程中不同 token 模态之间的信息交换。

"Our analyses examine how joint training with 3D language understanding affects the model's visual representations and novel-view generation capabilities." - 我们的分析研究了与 3D 语言理解的联合训练如何影响模型的视觉表示和新视角生成能力。

3.3 训练目标

我们使用面向新视角生成的流匹配目标来训练模型，并以 3D 问答和视觉定位（visual grounding）目标作为辅助监督。
训练采用多模态序列打包（sequence packing），并在独立样本之间阻断注意力。
总体目标为

ℒ total = λ gen ℒ gen + λ und ℒ und, ℒ und = ℒ qa + ℒ grd,

\mathcal{L}{\mathrm{total}}=\lambda{\mathrm{gen}}\mathcal{L}{\mathrm{gen}}+\lambda{\mathrm{und}}\mathcal{L}{\mathrm{und}},\qquad\mathcal{L}{\mathrm{und}}=\mathcal{L}{\mathrm{qa}}+\mathcal{L}{\mathrm{grd}},

(8)

其中 λ gen 和 λ und 用于对生成目标和辅助理解目标进行加权。

ally matched within each query based on 3D IoU.
→ （前文接续）在每个查询内基于 3D IoU 进行匹配。

→ 由此得到的匹配结果用于在 IoU 阈值为 0.25 和 0.5 时计算精确率（precision）、召回率（recall）和 F1。

A.3 Training and Architecture Details.
→ A.3 训练与架构细节。

Training configuration.
→ 训练配置。

We train our model using the Adam8bit optimizer (Dettmers et al., 2021) on 4 A100 GPUs, and accumulate gradients over 64 micro-steps per optimizer update.
→ 我们在 4 块 A100 GPU 上使用 Adam8bit 优化器（Dettmers et al., 2021）训练我们的模型，并在每次优化器更新时累积 64 个微步骤的梯度。

We train for 728 optimizer steps, corresponding to one epoch over the 3D language understanding data, with a linear warmup over 40 steps to 5×10^-5, followed by a constant learning rate (held at 5×10^-5).
→ 我们共训练 728 个优化器步数，相当于对 3D 语言理解数据进行一个 epoch 的训练，其中前 40 步进行线性预热至 5×10^-5，随后采用恒定学习率（保持在 5×10^-5）。

For novel-view generation, we use a timestep shift of 4.0 and apply conditioning dropout with a probability of 0.1 to each of the text and camera conditions.
→ 对于新视角生成，我们使用 4.0 的时间步偏移，并对文本条件和相机条件分别以 0.1 的概率应用条件丢弃。

For 3D visual grounding, we set the temperature hyperparameter τ_grd = 0.07.
→ 对于 3D 视觉定位，我们将温度超参数设置为 τ_grd = 0.07。

Following our backbone, we set the loss weights λ_gen and λ_und to 1.0 and 0.25, respectively.
→ 遵循我们的骨干网络设置，我们将损失权重 λ_gen 和 λ_und 分别设置为 1.0 和 0.25。

For a fair comparison, Lance-FT, Lance-FT + ViT, and Ask4NV are trained for the same number of optimizer steps and observe the same number of novel-view generation samples.
→ 为了公平比较，Lance-FT、Lance-FT + ViT 和 Ask4NV 均训练相同的优化器步数，并观察到相同数量的新视角生成样本。

Architectural details.
→ 架构细节。

We expand the input dimension of the pretrained projector P_vae from C_z to C̃_z = C_z + C_r to accommodate the concatenated ray representations.
→ 我们将预训练投影器 P_vae 的输入维度从 C_z 扩展到 C̃_z = C_z + C_r，以容纳拼接后的光线表示。

We retain the pretrained weights for the latent channels
→ 对于潜在通道，我们保留预训练权重

**多视角一致性。** 我们使用 MEt3R（Asim et al., 2025）的一个变体来评估多视角一致性，该变体将其原始的点图重建骨干网络替换为 VGGT-Ω（\Omega），并对所有评估方法使用相同的配置。对于每对生成的视图 I_a（I_{a}）和 I_b（I_{b}），我们提取密集 DINO 特征（Caron et al., 2021），并使用 FeatUp（Fu et al., 2024b）对其进行上采样。利用重建的点图，我们将两个特征图重投影并渲染到一个共同的视点，分辨率为 256×256（256\times 256）。我们在两个投影方向的有效重叠区域上计算它们的逐像素平均余弦相似度：

MEt3R(I_a, I_b) = 1 - (S_{a←b} + S_{b←a})/2,  (11)

其中 S_{a←b}（S_{a\leftarrow b}）表示在 I_a（I_{a}）视点下渲染的两个特征图之间的相似度。分数越低表示多视角一致性越强。我们评估所有无序的生成目标视图对并对其分数取平均，仅保留在两个方向上均具有有效分数的视图对。对于所有几何指标，我们首先在每个场景内对有效的元组级分数取平均，然后跨场景取平均，从而赋予每个场景相同的权重。

"generation training uses only Re10K." - 生成训练仅使用 Re10K。

"Appendix B" - 附录 B

"Related Work" - 相关工作

"B.1 Novel View Generation" - B.1 新视角生成

"Novel-view synthesis (NVS) aims to predict the appearance of a scene from unseen viewpoints given observed views." - 新视角合成（NVS）旨在根据已观测的视图，预测场景在未见过视角下的外观。

"Reconstruction-based approaches, such as NeRF (Mildenhall et al., 2021) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (Kerbl et al., 2023), model scenes using 3D representations from which novel views are rendered." - 基于重建的方法，如 NeRF（Mildenhall 等，2021）和 3D Gaussian Splatting（Kerbl 等，2023），使用 3D 表示对场景进行建模，并从中渲染出新视角。

"Recent advances in generative modeling (Ho et al., 2020; Rombach et al., 2022; Peebles and Xie, 2023) have enabled novel-view generation from sparse observations by leveraging learned visual priors." - 生成建模的最新进展（Ho 等，2020；Rombach 等，2022；Peebles 和 Xie，2023）通过利用学习到的视觉先验，实现了从稀疏观测中生成新视角。

"To adapt these priors to 3D scenes, existing methods explore camera conditioning (Gao et al., 2024; Sargent et al., 2024), geometric guidance (Cao et al., 2025; Li et al., 2025), cross-view consistency (Kwon et al., 2025; Müller et al., 2024), and geometry-aware latent representations (Jang et al., 2026)." - 为了使这些先验适应 3D 场景，现有方法探索了相机条件化（Gao 等，2024；Sargent 等，2024）、几何引导（Cao 等，2025；Li 等，2025）、跨视图一致性（Kwon 等，2025；Müller 等，2024）以及几何感知的潜在表示（Jang 等，2026）。
